## Document 011: 1802台玻 群益

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 10 月 21 日

1 群益投顧 
台玻 (1802 TT ) 10/20/2026 首戰台灣電路板產業國際博覽會 (TPCA Show 2026) 。本次
會 中 主 要 展 出 其 高 階 玻 纖 布 。 當 前 以 Low Dk1/Dk2 為 玻 纖 布 事 業 群 主 要 營 收來源。
Low CTE 則已於 2025 年 底 開 始 小 量 出 貨 ， 目 前 產 品 出 貨 量 已 較2026 年 初 顯 著 增 加 。
當前營收比重為 Low Dk2>Low Dk1=Low CTE 。鑒於 Low Dk 1/Dk2 產 品 出 貨 動 能 佳，
Low CTE 營 收 貢 獻 逐步提升下，毛利表現亦將持續走揚。產品供需結構尚處緊缺階
段， 公司 亦 有 望 部 分 反 映 報 價 。 給 予 投 資 建 議Buy ， 目 標 價 81 元。

研 究 員 ： 蔡 季 衡   
A52320@cim.capital.com.tw

台 玻 首 戰 TPCA Show ：  
台玻 10/20/2026 首戰 台 灣 電 路 板 產 業 國 際 博 覽 會(TPCA Show 2026) 。本次
會中 主 要 展 出 其 高 階 玻 纖 布 ， 包 括 TD(Low Dk1) 、 TDA(Low Dk2) 、 TS(Low 
CTE) 、 Q (Q 布 )等 ， 並 詳 解 各 產 品 特 性 及 運 用 範 疇 。 
一、 聚 焦 於 Low Dk 、 Low CTE 玻纖布 ：  
TD(Low Dk1) 、 TDA(Low Dk2) 具 低 介 電 損 耗 並 於 維 持 一 定 電 性 水 準 下 ， 提
供 較 佳 產 品 穩 定 性 。 目 前 主 要 應 用 於 電 子 產 業 及PCB 產 業 、IC 載 板 、 先 進
封 裝 材 料 、 航 空 及 汽 車 領 域 等 。 
TS(Low CTE) 具 低 熱 膨 脹 係 數 特 性， 並 具 備 高機 械 韌 性(剛性 )、 絕 佳 電 性 絕
緣 及 先 進 樹 酯兼 容 性。目 前 主 要 應 用 於 5G/6G 、微 波 通 訊 設 備、高 速 電 路 板、
高頻 PCB 、 光 模 塊 、 高 速 交 換 器 及資 料 傳 輸 等。  
二 、 目 前 營 收 比 重 為 Low Dk2>Low Dk1=Low CTE ：  
E 布 轉 至 中 國 產 銷 ，以 Low Dk 1/Dk2 為 當 前 玻 纖 布 事 業 群 主 要 營 收來 源 。
Low CTE 則 已於 2025 年 底 開 始 小 量 出 貨 ， 目 前產 品 出 貨 量 已 較 2026 年初
顯 著 增 加，預 期 營 收 佔 比 將 於 2027 年 陸 續 提 升，並 於 2028 年 伴隨 近 年 陸 續
擴建 之 新 產 能 開 出 後 ，顯 著 提 高 。鑒 於 目 前 下 游 終 端 需 求 仍 以 Low Dk 產品
為主力， 研究部 預期 2027 年 Low Dk2 營 收 比 重 仍 將 高 於 Low Dk1 及 Low 
CTE 。 當 前 營 收 比 重 為 Low Dk2>Low Dk1=Low CTE 。 另 外 ， 台 玻 目 前 亦嘗
試 生 產 測 試 Low Dk3( 三代布 )， 後 續 若 生 產 良 率 過 關 ， 亦 將送 樣 驗 證 。 
三 、 不 急 於 產 品 漲 價 ， 堅 守 優 質 客 戶 連 結：  
台 玻 生 產 研 發 玻 纖 布數 十 年，始 終 秉 持 於 維 繫 良 好 客 戶 關 係，並 以 客 戶 需 求
為 營 運 宗 旨 。 雖 2025~2026 年以來 E 布 報 價 倍 數 增 長 ， 惟 基 於多年來與 客
戶 合作 建立 之 信任 及當前產品利潤貢獻尚屬可控範圍，故而 預期將 不急於
大 幅 反 映 產 品 報 價 ， 但 亦 不 排 除 部 分 調 整 產 品 報 價 。 
四 、 具 備 Q 布 生 產 技 術 及 能 力：  
台 玻 於 本 次 TPCA Show 上 ， 已 展 出 Q 布 玻 纖 紗 ，顯示 其 Q 布 技 術 儲 備 ， 且
已 具 備 接 單 出 貨 能 力 。 惟 觀 察 AI 伺 服 器 規 格 調 整，預期 2027 年 產 品 需 求
仍以 Low Dk 系 列 玻 纖 布 為 主 ， 大 量 搭 載 Q 布 尚 早 。 不 同 材 料 規 格 之 玻 纖
布 ， 其 製 程 方 式雖 雷 同 ， 惟 配 方 均 有 差 異 ，窯 爐 使 用 亦 不 同 。 因 此 ， 台 玻 將
視 下 游 廠 商 產 品 需 求 ， 決 議 Q 布 生 產 時 程。

2 群益投顧 
五、 研 究 部 預 估台玻 2027 年稅後 EPS 4.37 元 ：  
台玻 09/2026 營收 49.68 億 元 ，MoM+8.18% 、YoY+34.11% ，主因 Low Dk1/Low 
Dk2 等 高 階 玻 纖 布 出 貨 動 能 較 08/2026 提 升。若 以 玻 纖 布 營 收 比 重 40~45%，
且高階玻纖布佔其中 40~42% 推估， 09/2026 高階玻纖布營收貢獻約
MoM+7~9% 。 3Q26 營收 合計 約 141.41 億 元 ， QoQ+17.24% 、 YoY+31.82% ，
亦 優 於 原 預 期 之 138.80 億元， QoQ+15.08% 、 YoY+29.40% 。  
展望 2027 ~2028 年 ， 預 期 2027 年 Low Dk1/Dk2 玻 纖 布 出 貨 量 仍 將 較 2026
年 顯 著 提 升，Low CTE 營 收 貢 獻 亦 將 持 續 走 揚，2028 年 伴 隨 新 產 能 開 出 後，
有望趕上 Low Dk2 。 目 前 台 玻 Low CTE 產 品 已 陸 續 取 得 國 內 外 大 廠 認 證 ，
訂 單 能 見 度 亦 已 達 2027 年，且 2028 年 訂 單 洽 談 順 暢。因 此，供 應 鏈 產 品 需
求若 無 較 大 變 化 ，2028 年 玻 纖 布 事 業 群 仍 將持 續 成 長 。 
日本大廠 Nittobo 多 次 公 開 表 示 ， 雖 2028 年產能 倍增 ，惟 T 布 市 場 需 求 已
非 當 前 公 司 擴 產 速 度 所 能 消 化 ， 顯 示 產 品 需 求 強 勁 。 研 究 部預 估 台 玻 4Q26
底 ~2027 年 玻 纖 布 營 收 比 重 有 望 超 越 平 板 玻 璃 和 玻 璃 器 皿 。預估 2027 年營
收 649.42 億 元 ， YoY +26.21 %， 毛利率 32.01% ， 稅 後 淨 利 127.17 億 元 ，
YoY +90.06 %， 稅後 EPS 4.37 元 。  
投 資 建 議 ：  
台玻 10/20/2026 首 戰 台 灣 電 路 板 產 業 國 際 博 覽 會(TPCA Show 2026) 。本次
會 中 主 要 展 出 其 高 階 玻 纖 布。 當 前 以 Low Dk1/Dk2 為 玻 纖 布 事 業 群 主 要 營
收 來源。 Low CTE 則已於 2025 年 底 開 始 小 量 出 貨 ， 目 前 產 品 出 貨 量 已 較
2026 年 初 顯 著 增 加。 當 前 營 收 比 重 為 Low Dk2>Low Dk1=Low CTE 。 鑒於
Low Dk 1/Dk2 產 品 出 貨 動 能 佳 ，Low CTE 營 收 貢 獻 逐 步 提 升 下 ， 毛 利 表 現
亦 將 持 續 走 揚。產 品 供 需 結 構 尚 處 緊 缺 階 段，公 司 亦 有 望 部 分 反 映 報 價。給
予 投 資 建 議 Buy ， 目 標 價 81 元。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 10 月 21 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 110: 261020_daiwa_ZDT

Source PDF: all report pdf/261020_daiwa_ZDT.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Background: Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT), the world’s largest PCB 
manufacturer by revenue, generated revenue of TWD182.5bn in 2025. 
Historically focused on consumer electronics and smartphones, the 
company is shifting towards higher-value AI-server PCBs (HLC/iHDI), 
optical-module PCBs (mSAP), and IC substrates (ABF/BT). These 
businesses accounted for 21.6% of its revenue in 1H26, up from 11.7% in 
2025. Management targets this ratio to reach c.25% in 2026 and c.30% in 
2027. To support this shift, ZDT has 22 fabs under construction and plans 
to build nine more, primarily for mSAP/HDI, HLC, and ABF substrates.

Highlights: ZDT’s “One ZDT strategy” is gradually bearing fruit. Since 
2018, management has pursued its “One ZDT strategy” to broaden the 
product portfolio – including HLC, substrates, mSAP/HDI, rigid-flex and 
SLP – by leveraging its strengths in FPCB and consumer HDI. This 
strategy is beginning to pay off, and management expects its fast-growing 
segments, including mSAP HDI for optical transceivers, AI-server HDI and 
ABF/BT substrates, to gain momentum from this year. Management 
expects revenue from these segments to increase by c.150% YoY and 
account for c.25% of total revenue (or c.TWD50bn) in 2026, and to further 
increase by 100% YoY to TWD100bn in 2027. ZDT targets these fast-
growing segments to reach 30% of its revenue mix in 2027 and c.45-50% 
in 2030. Given current progress, the 2030 target could be reached in 2028. 
For mSAP HDI, management expects the 800G/1.6T transceiver product 
mix to shift to 7:3 in 2027, from 5:5 in 2026, and aims to become the largest 
supplier of optical-transceiver HDI in 2027. In AI-server HDI, we expect the 
2 leading ASIC vendors to gradually shift from pure HLC to thick HDI 
boards – a combination of HLC and HDI – in their next-generation designs, 
with ZDT a potential beneficiary. This could drive c.3x growth in ZDT’s 
business in 2027.

Product mix to support gross margin expansion. Margin expansion is 
increasingly driven by a favourable product mix. Gross margin reached 
22.4% in 1H26 (vs. 16.5% in 1H25). Management indicated that optical-
transceiver HDIs currently have the highest gross margin, at over 40%, 
followed by IC substrates, with ABF margins potentially reaching 50-60% 
and BT margins around 30% as a long-term target. Server HLC margins 
are around 20-30%, while HDI margins should be higher given their greater 
process complexity. With margins in traditional businesses at c.20%, ZDT 
expects growth in the newer segments to drive further margin expansion.

Valuation: The stock is trading at 36/19x PER for 2026/27E based on 
Bloomberg consensus. It’s past 3-year PER range is 10-30x.

20 October 2026

Business transformation bears fruit

➢ Key beneficiary of growth in optical transceiver & AI server HDI demand  
➢ Aggressive substrate capacity strategy paying off in current conditions   
➢ Margin expansion on fast-growing segments (HDI and substrates)

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Implications to our coverage: If next-gen AI server racks do not adopt PTFE-based
CCL, we think the outcome would skew positive for EMC and TUC but slightly

negative for Shengyi. We expect the material choice to be confirmed by mid-2027 at
the latest. Additionally, with EMC expecting its substrate CCL capacity to more than
double by end-2028, Unimicron and other substrate makers gain an alternative
supply source, which we view as positive. Substrate CCL carries higher gross
margins than PCB CCL, so a higher contribution from this business should improve
EMC’s profitability as S572T ramps from 4Q26. On ABF substrate, lead times of at

least two years for key equipment such as EPM’s ABF laminators imply that new
greenfield ABF substrate capacity can only ramp from 2029 onwards. This aligns
with our view that ABF substrate undersupply will deepen in 2027-28. Substrate
sizes going to 230mm x 230mm by 2028, implying only 4 substrates per panel, is
also supportive of our positive view on demand. This would make it very difficult to
control yield, as one area of defect on the panel would imply 25% yield loss.

PTFE CCL: Adoption of glass cloth-free PTFE CCLs in next-gen AI server systems
was the topic investors asked about most at the booths. As we have written
previously (here and here), pure PTFE CCLs could deliver the best signal integrity
(SI), making them the preferred solution for midplane/switch PCBs for some
customers. However, their processability at PCB makers remains a challenge for
now, particularly for high-layer-count boards. In addition, multilayer boards (MLBs)

using PTFE require special high-temperature presses capable of reaching close to
400°C, as standard vacuum presses are insufficient. In response, Taiwanese CCL
suppliers are also exploring other materials that could deliver M10-and-beyond
performance, including hydrocarbon (HC)-based, HC+PTFE hybrid and
benzocyclobutene (BCB)-based CCLs. We expect the CCL material for the next-gen
AI server rack to be confirmed by mid-2027 at the latest.
Other key takeaways can be found on the following page.

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
PTFE offers the best SI but is harder to process; TW CCL makers are less bullish
on PTFE and are also exploring HC, HC+PTFE hybrid & BCB.
Thin E-glass cloth shortages and price hikes to last through 1H27, at least, as
looms stay scarce and lines shift to LDK/T-glass.
EPM’s ABF laminators (95%+ global share) are a key bottleneck to ABF substrate

expansion, with 2+ year lead times and no new greenfield capacity until 2029.
Glass core substrate is likely not commercially viable in the next 2-3 years, with
crack inspection after ABF lamination currently being problematic.
October 26, 2026 01:42 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Substrate CCL: S572T, the most advanced substrate CCL on display at EMC’s booth,
enters mass production in 4Q26, with volumes expected to increase sequentially
throughout 2027. EMC expects its substrate CCL capacity to more than double from
current levels by end-2028 and said it will have multiple major ABF substrate
customers. It added that it has qualified additional low-CTE glass sources to ensure
sufficient supply.
Glass cloth shortage: Several CCL suppliers still cite thin E-glass cloth as the key

material bottleneck. They generally expect the shortage to persist through 1H27
with continued price hikes, and one supplier believes it could last through late 2027.
In their view, the tightness in electronic-grade glass cloth stems from insufficient
supply of Japanese-made looms, as well as from existing glass cloth suppliers either
prioritizing in-house demand or converting production lines to LDK1/2 and T-glass
cloth to meet strong AI demand. One glass cloth supplier noted it remains reluctant

to adopt non-Toyota looms given the higher defect rates and repair costs.
ABF laminator: We also visited the booth of Eternal Materials (1717.TW, Not
Covered), whose subsidiary Eternal Precision Mechanics (EPM, 7795.TW, Not
Covered) holds 95%+ global market share in vacuum laminators used to laminate
ABF onto substrate cores. EPM’s ABF laminators are now one of the key
bottlenecks in ABF substrate capacity expansion. Our checks with EPM suggest
equipment lead times have stretched to at least two years and EPM’s new

greenfield capacity will not come online until 2029; until then, EPM can only
debottleneck by optimizing existing capacity and leasing idle capacity from third
parties. For context, a new ABF substrate plant may require at least 10 sets of EPM’s
most advanced three-stage vacuum laminator and pre-track machine, depending on
factory layout.
Glass core substrate: Microcracks and the resulting package reliability issues are
currently the two key technical bottlenecks for glass core substrates. Existing

automated optical inspection (AOI) tools struggle to detect and identify microcracks
in the glass core, and laminating multiple layers of Ajinomoto Build-up Film (ABF)
onto it makes inspection impossible, according to Shinko. One equipment supplier
also flagged metallization of high-aspect-ratio through-glass vias (TGVs) as very
challenging. We understand this supplier is not involved in copper pillar mass
transfer, an alternative approach to TGV metallization.

OSAT 展望樂觀，SOIC 機台具技術優勢 
 2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT:2026 年弘塑產能擴充一倍 ，1Q26 已
經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ， 目前外包比例33%，
未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外包產能受
限，同業也在搶外包產能。2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT，今年晶
圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年 OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，
SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026 差不多略增，2028
才會明顯大增，主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度。 
 2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量會很明顯放大:PLP 及 CoPoS 設備機
台單價很高，初估有機會是 CoWoS 翻倍，PLP 進度比較快，PLP
規格弘塑 310*310、510*515、600*600 都有，再大到 700 以上就不
做了，晶圓代工客戶規格是 310*310 但目前進度比較慢，還在實驗
線階段。力成 510*515 進度最快，4Q26 會拉第一批機台，2027 隨
力成購置的友達廠進度持續拉機台，2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量
會很明顯放大。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 3000 元：基本面無變化，預估數字也沒做調
整，故維持買進評等 ，預估 2026 年 EPS 64.81 元，2027 年 EPS 93.97
元，目標價 3000 元。(2027 PER 30x)

弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商 
 弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商 
弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商，主要利基產品為半導體後段濕製程設備，並延
伸相關化學品、檢測設備代理及軟體解決方案。1H26 產品結構可區分為機台檢
測產品(65%)、化學產品(20%)、代理設備(15%)。2013 年合併化學品公司添鴻
(CLC)、2018 年底合併通路商佳霖(ClC)、併購資訊系統廠太引(Tyne)，投資美
國 X-Ray 檢測公司 SVXR。 客戶為國內半導體 、 光電 、記憶體及面板產業大廠，
包括台積電、矽品、日月光、力成、星科金朋、精材、聯電、茂矽、采鈺、穏
懋、中芯、宏達等。

2Q26 毛 利率 39.37%， 低於預
期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
 2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，低於預期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
弘塑 2Q26 EPS 11.18 元，低於元富預估的 12.34 元，獲利達成率 91%。本業
來看，2Q26 營收 17.27 億元，+8%QOQ，+6%YOY，2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，
較 1Q26 回升 5.1 個百分點，然仍低於預估的 41%，主要是外包成本及急單增
加影響部分毛利率，預期 3Q26 毛利率持續向上改善，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元。

弘塑 3Q26 營收 22.67 億元，+31.3%QOQ，+51.8%YOY，營收達成率 98%，
略低於預期，預估 3Q26 毛利率 41%，預估 3Q26 EPS 16.47 元。

營運逐季成長，OSA T展望樂觀

營運逐季成長，OSA T展望樂觀 
2026 年弘塑擴產一倍 ，1Q26 已經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ，
目前外包比例 33%，未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外
包產能受限 ， 同業也在搶外包產能 。2027 年擴產再 50-100%， 以租用廠房為主。
2027 主要成長動能還是 OSAT，今年晶圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年
OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026
差不多略增 ，2028 才會明顯大增 ， 主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度，
SOIC 機台種類 6-7 種。

## Document 151: 35 岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了

Source PDF: all report pdf/35 岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做成上线系统的⼈。
摘要：
数据显⽰，全球科技公司年内已宣布裁员近  20 万⼈。与此同时，有⼀个  AI 岗位的招聘量却在
⼀年⾥暴涨了  7 倍多，薪资中位数折合⼈⺠币约  130 万元。

OpenAI 、 Anthropic 、微软、亚⻢逊、⾕歌，⼏家巨头前后砸下近百亿美元，有的开新公司，有
的建新部⻔，抢的都是这类⼈才。最反常识的是：这个岗位最稀缺的本事，恰恰不是写代码。

Anthropic ：培养  1 万个 “ 把  AI 送进企业的⼈ ”

10 ⽉  2 ⽇， Claude 的开发商  Anthropic 宣布投⼊  1 亿美元成⽴ “Claude 前沿学院 ” （ Claude
Frontier Academy ），⽬标是在  2027 年底前培养  1 万名 “ 前沿部署⼯程师 ” 。

⾸批学员是从合作伙伴中选出的在职⼯程师，来⾃埃森哲、摩根⼠丹利、诺和诺德和⼏家顶级
咨询公司。培训分两段：先是  4 天线下集训，从⼀次 “ 模拟企业部署 ” 开始；考核通过后，学员回
到⾃⼰公司，带⼀个真实项⽬做  12 周实战。⾸批认证预计在  2027 年初完成。

在  Anthropic 看来，企业落地  AI 的瓶颈不在模型，⽽在于缺少能推动变⾰的⼈。负责这个项⽬
的⾼管  Steve Corfield 说，客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做
成上线系统的⼈。

这只是今年  AI 巨头抢⼈⼤战中的最新⼀例。

砸下百亿美元，巨头们都在抢同⼀种⼈才

今年  5 ⽉， OpenAI 成⽴了专⻔帮企业落地  AI 的 “OpenAI 部署公司 ” ，由  TPG 领投，⾸轮融资
超过  40 亿美元。同⽉， Anthropic 与⿊⽯、 Hellman & Friedman 、⾼盛合资成⽴了⼀家  AI 服
务公司，各⽅初始投资约  15 亿美元。

6 ⽉底到  7 ⽉初，亚⻢逊  AWS 和微软先后出⼿： AWS 投⼊  10 亿美元，组建⾃⼰的部署⼯程
团队；微软投⼊  25 亿美元成⽴新部⻔，把约  6000 名⼯程专家派驻到客⼾企业。 9 ⽉，⾕歌云
和埃森哲成⽴联合业务部⻔，计划培训最多  1000 名埃森哲⼯程师，专⻔派驻客⼾现场。

过去这些公司⽐的是谁的模型更强、参数更⼤、跑分更⾼，如今却不约⽽同地把钱砸向同⼀个
⽅向：派⼈进客⼾公司，把  AI 真正⽤起来。

这类⼈才在业内有个统⼀的叫法：FDE （ Forward Deployed Engineer ），中⽂常译作“ 前线部
署⼯程师 ”。

招聘量三年猛涨 30 倍，年薪中位数超百万元

熟悉美股的读者，对这个词的源头不会陌⽣。 FDE 最早是  Palantir （帕兰泰尔）在⼗多年前系
统化推⾏的⼀种做法：⼯程师不坐在总部写通⽤产品，⽽是常驻客⼾现场，和客⼾⽤同⼀套数
据、扛同⼀个业务指标，把模糊的需求、分散的数据和落不了地的⽬标，⼀点点变成真正能⽤
的系统。

当年很多⼈觉得这种模式太重、太慢，不像⼀家软件公司该⼲的事。今天回头看，这恰恰是
Palantir 最难被复制的护城河： 2026 年第⼆季度， Palantir 营收  19.35 亿美元，同⽐增⻓
93% ，其中美国商业收⼊同⽐增⻓  149% 。公司⾸席执⾏官  Alex Karp 有⼀个在业内被反复引⽤
的判断：⼤模型当然重要，但⾄少在未来⼏年⾥，价值在于落地实施。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

据劳动⼒市场分析机构  Lightcast 统计， 2023 年全美  FDE 招聘⼴告不到  200 条， 2025 年约
1200 条， 2026 年仅前九个⽉就已超过  7300 条。这些⼴告标出的薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元，
⽐  2023 年⾼出  40% ，是全美所有招聘⼴告薪资中位数的三倍多。 Indeed 的数据也指向同⼀个
⽅向：美国  FDE 职位从  2025 年  4 ⽉的  643 个增⾄  2026 年  4 ⽉的  5330 个，⼀年涨了  7 倍
多。

值得注意的是，今年前九个⽉发布  FDE 招聘⼴告最多的雇主中，德勤、埃森哲等咨询公司排在
了⾕歌等科技巨头之前。可⻅  FDE 已不只是  AI 公司的内部技术岗位，任何想把  AI ⽤进业务的
企业都需要这样的⼈。

需要说明的是， FDE 不等于 “ 驻场外包 ” 。外包是甲⽅说什么就做什么；FDE 则要先判断⼀件事
该不该⽤  AI 、⽤哪个模型、花多少钱、怎么证明有效，再把它真正落地上线。它要解决的是企
业⾥的⼀个⽼难题：懂技术的不懂业务，懂业务的不懂技术。

AI 落地难在业务，稀缺的恰恰是懂业务的⼈

很多⼈看到这⾥会想：这是年轻程序员的机会，我不写代码，也过了转⾏的年纪。

但不妨看看  Anthropic 怎么描述⼀个好的  FDE ：熟悉  Agent 系统、擅⻓解决问题、善于沟通、
有⼯程经验。这四条⾥，有⼀半在技术之外。

过去两年，⼏乎每家企业都买过  AI 产品、做过  AI 试点，但真正跑进⽇常业务的远⽐宣传的
少。⼏组被业内⼴泛引⽤的数据或许能说明⼀些问题：标普全球的调查显⽰，企业  AI 项⽬的弃
⽤率⼀年内从  17% 升到了  42%； Gartner 预计，到  2027 年超过四成的  Agent 项⽬会被取消；
IDC 与联想  2025 年的⼀份报告则显⽰，88% 的  AI 概念验证项⽬最终没有投⼊⽣产。

问题往往不出在模型上 —— 演⽰时⼀切顺利，⼀进⼊真实业务，就会碰上脏数据、⽼系统、权
限审批、员⼯习惯，以及 “ 出了错谁负责 ” 。毕⻢威的⼀位⾸席技术架构师举过⼀个例⼦：⼀个应
⽤先花了⼏个⽉梳理需求和业务背景，真正⽤  AI 开发只⽤了⼤约⼀个⽉。

FDE 真正值钱的那⼀半能⼒不是编程，⽽是懂业务。⼀份合同哪⼏条要紧，⼀张报表哪个数字
会出事，⼀个审批流程卡在哪⼀步，哪件事交给机器做会让⽼板睡不着觉，这些  AI 替代不了，
反⽽是在⾏业⾥⼲了⼗⼏、⼆⼗年的⼈最清楚。

你缺少的不是经验，⽽是把经验交给  AI 去执⾏的那⼀步：知道今天的  AI 能做什么、不能做什
么、该怎么搭、要花多少钱、出了错怎么兜底。

我们这次的训练营，就是为这⼀步准备的。

两天⾛完  AI 落地最后⼀公⾥

华尔街⻅闻⾸期「 FDE 实战训练营」将于  11 ⽉  14 ⽇⾄  15 ⽇在上海开班。这是⼀个线下实战
营，限量  50 席，在两天时间⾥，带你从零做出⼀个真正能⽤、能上线的  AI Agent 。

先说说  Agent （智能体）是什么。很多⼈对  AI 的印象还停留在 “ 问⼀句答⼀句 ” 的聊天上，但
Agent 不⼀样：你交给它⼀件事，它会⾃⼰查资料、打开软件、读写⽂件、交出结果，做错了还
能⾃⼰返⼯。巨头们派  FDE 进企业，部署的正是这种技术。

两天训练营共  12 个模块，从模型怎么选、价格怎么算，⼀直讲到评测、成本优化和安全上线，
都是⼤⼚把  AI 做上线时真正在⽤的⽅法。主讲讲师  Right 曾任腾讯⾼级算法⼯程师，主导过微
信核⼼  AI Agent 项⽬的算法设计和规模化落地，在  Agent 系统设计、模型训练、 Harness 架构
⽅向有深厚积累。他还在  NeurIPS 、 ACL 等顶级会议发表过多篇  Agent ⽅向论⽂，相关⼯作被
Google DeepMind 引⽤。

课程适合谁
企业主和管理者：想弄清  AI 到底能替公司做哪些事、每件事花多少钱、⻛险在哪⾥

### Page 3

⾦融、法务、财务、运营、咨询等专业⼈⼠：⼿上有⼤量重复⼯作，想亲⼿做⼀个⼯具交给
AI
想在  AI 时代给⾃⼰多留⼀项硬技能的⼈

三⼗年前，先学会⽤  Excel 的那批⼈，在后来的⼆⼗年⾥少熬了⽆数个夜。
今天巨头们花百亿美元在抢懂业务、⼜会⽤  AI 的⼈，⽽你已经有了⼀半。剩下那⼀半，就在这
两天⾥补⻬。

点击海报，锁定早⻦席位

### Page 8

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

### Page 1

林渝珊 
(886-2)2563-1666 
stella.lin@firstsec.com.tw

藍新資訊(6938) 
買進, TP 120 (潛在報酬 76.47%)  
買進 
前一日(10/22)收盤價:78 
承銷價:68 
Target Price:120

考量藍新資訊具備大型主機與異質平台整合技術優勢 ， 長期深耕政
府、金融及大型企業客戶，核心系統更換成本高，建立較高客戶黏
著度與技術進入門檻。短期受惠政府專案驗收旺季及企業 AI 基礎
建設需求增加，營收可望維持雙位數成長；中長期隨金融核心系統
汰換、資安升級 及大型專案承接能力提升，將進一步擴大營運規
模。此外，透過「專案建置帶動維運」之商業模式，持續累積高毛
利經常性收入，有助降低專案認列波動並提升獲利穩定性。綜合以
上 ， 研究部預估藍新資訊2027 年營收為 9.64 億元 ，Y+18.08%，
毛利率 29.08% ， Y+0.23ppts ， 營 業 利 益 率 13.88% ，
Y+0.68ppts， 稅後純益1.10 億元 ，Y+24.40%，EPS 為 7.50 元。
考量公司具備大型核心系統整合技術優勢 ， 且維運合約具備高度客
戶黏著度，正面看待中長期營運成長潛力，參考同業本益比區間約
在 10~20 倍，給予 16 倍本益比評價，目標價為 120 元，投資建
議為買進。 
 藍新資訊(6938.TT)公司簡介 
藍新資訊(6938.TT)成立於 2014 年 4 月，於 2024 年 1 月登錄興
櫃，並於 2026 年 10 月 23 日轉掛牌上櫃交易，前身為藍新科技
系統整合事業部，核心經營及技術團隊主要來自 IBM 金融與系統
服務事業群。公司為台灣專業資訊系統整合服務商，專注於大型企
業核心系統、雲端基礎建設、資訊安全及數位韌性等領域，具備跨
品牌 、 異質平台整合能力 ， 可整合IBM、HPE、Dell EMC、Cisco、
NetApp、Oracle 及 VMware 等國際大廠軟硬體，提供系統架構
規劃、設備建置、系統移轉及維運服務。營收結構方面，2025 年
商品銷售及系統整合占比約 61%，整合技術與服務約 39%，其中
技術服務涵蓋系統維護、7×24 技術支援及專業人力派駐，近三年

營運現況 
大型系統建置專案帶動營收成長 ， 低毛利硬體占比提升壓抑獲利率 
藍新資訊 1H26 營收為 3.29 億元，Y+22.13%，主要受惠大型系
統整合及數位轉型專案陸續完成建置 ， 包括核心主機設備 、ERP 系
統儲存設備與伺服器汰換、大數據資料庫及 AI 應用專案。產品組
合為商品銷售及系統整合 56.24%，整合技術與服務 43.51%，受
惠既有客戶維護合約延續及新建置專案帶動後續維運需求 ， 提供穩
定經常性收入。獲利方面，因大型數位轉型及主機建置專案毛利率
較低，加上記憶體零組件採購成本上升，使毛利率降至 30.05%，

未來展望 
政府專案進入旺季，AI 基礎建設及應用需求帶動下半年營收成長 
展望 2H26，藍新資訊營運可望優於上半年，主要受惠資訊服務業
進入傳統專案驗收旺季，加上政府預算審查完成，先前推進之大型
數位轉型、雙資料中心及資安建置專案可望於下半年陸續驗收，帶
動第四季營收認列加速 。 另一方面 ，AI 相關業務營收占比已由 2025
年約 3%提升至 1H26 的 8%， 預期下半年將持續成長 。 獲利方面，
儘管大型硬體專案出貨比重提高可能壓抑毛利率 ，然而隨高毛利維
護維運收入穩定增加，預期整體毛利率可維持約 30%水準。綜合
以上 ， 研究部預估藍新資訊2026 年營收為 8.17 億元 ，Y+20.69%，
毛利率 28.85%，Y-2.19ppts， 營業利益率13.20%，Y-1.04ppts，
稅後純益 0.88 億元，Y+14.19%，EPS 為 6.65 元。

大型專案承接能力提升，金融核心系統汰換與資安需求支撐成長 
展望 2027 年，隨上櫃後資本規模及市場能見度提升，可望強化大
型專案承接能力，未來將持續爭取更大型系統整合案件。業務方
面，金融機構核心交易系統面臨主機汰換及雙活備援升級需求，公
司憑藉 IBM UNIX/Linux 大型主機平台整合經驗 ， 有利爭取相關建
置商機；此外，政府及企業持續強化零信任架構、身分存取管理與
資安防護，預期相關系統整合需求將延續，支撐中長期營收成長。

AI 應用深化帶動新業務需求，維運合約增加支撐獲利結構改善 
隨企業 AI 應用由概念驗證轉向正式部署 ， 對GPU 算力平台 、 企業
私有化 LLM、資料治理等整合服務需求可望增加，帶動 AI 相關業
務持續成長。獲利方面，公司採取「專案建置帶動維運」之商業模
式，隨前期大型專案陸續完工，預期將增加後續維護維運合約需
求 ， 搭配逾90%的合約存續率 ， 有助高毛利經常性收入持續累積。
此外，公司規劃技術人員明年擴編約 8%至 10%，以支援新增專案
及維運需求，預期隨服務收入規模擴大，有助改善整體獲利結構。
綜合以上，研究 部預估藍新資訊 2027 年營收為 9.64 億元，
Y+18.08%， 毛利率29.08%，Y+0.23ppts， 營業利益率13.88%，
Y+0.68ppts，稅後純益 1.10 億元，Y+24.40%，EPS 為 7.50 元。

結論與建議
考量藍新資訊具備大型主機與異質平台整合技術優勢 ， 長期深耕政
府、金融及大型企業客戶，核心系統更換成本高，建立較高客戶黏
著度與技術進入門檻。短期受惠政府專案驗收旺季及企業 AI 基礎
建設需求增加，營收可望維持雙位數成長；中長期隨金融核心系統
汰換、資安升級及大型專案承接能力提升，將進一步擴大營運規
模。此外，透過「專案建置帶動維運」之商業模式，持續累積高毛
利經常性收入，有助降低專案認列波動並提升獲利穩定性。綜合以
上，研究部預估藍新資訊2027 年營收為 9.64 億元，Y+18.08%，
毛利率 29.08%，Y+0.23ppts， 營業利益率13.88%，Y+0.68ppts，
稅後純益 1.10 億元，Y+24.40%，EPS 為 7.50 元。考量公司具備
大型核心系統整合技術優勢，且維運合約具備高度客戶黏著度，正

面看待中長期營 運成長潛力，參考同業本益比區間約在 10~20
倍，給予 16 倍本益比評價，目標價為 120 元，投資建議為買進。

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

## Document 275: Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds

Source PDF: all report pdf/Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Compelling risk-reward in 
US 10-year bonds  
Summary 
Event: The US 10 -year government bond yield has surged 
past 5.0% to a 19-year high and has now entered our three -
month forecast range of 5.0-5.25%. At the time of writing, the 
10-year yield is trading at 5.2%, its highest since June 2007’s 
high of 5.32%. The next resistance levels are the 2002 high 
of 5.47% and the 2001 high of 5.53%.  
We view the risk/reward for the US 10-year government 
bond after the recent yield spike as  compelling and we 
are initiating a long opportunistic idea at the current 
yield. As the table on the right shows, if the 10 -year yield 
rises to 6%, investors would face less than 1% in negative 
total returns over a one-year period, whereas if the yield falls 
below 5.0%, they would gain at least 6% in total returns.  
While we continue to prefer the 3-7-year bond maturity range 
for income generation (the US 5-year bond yield is also 
above 5%), and broadly prefer corporate over government 
bonds amid solid corporate fundamentals, the US 10 -year 
government bond yield has finally reached reasonable levels 
for investors looking for slightly longer maturities. 
Rationale: We expect the US 10-year government bond yield 
to ease to 4.75-5.0% over a 12-month period due to: 
Easing oil prices and inflation over next 12 months: While 
the yield is likely to remain elevated in the next three months 
due to elevated oil prices caused by geopolitical uncertainty, 
heavy AI investment-driven bond issuance and a resilient US 
economy, it is likely to ease towards the 4.75 -5.0% range 
over 12  months as oil prices moderate either due to a 
resolution in the Middle East or a rerouting of Middle East 
energy supplies and as the impact of tariffs fade.  
We expect s hallower Fed rate hikes than market s: We 
expect a total of 50bps of Fed rate hikes by June 2027, before 
a cut in H2 as US inflation eventually eases towards the Fed’s 
2% target. This  rate trajectory is shallower than c. 95bps of 
further hikes priced by markets over the next 12 months. 
 The benchmark US 10-year government bond yield has 
risen to its highest since 2007, offering medium-term 
investors a compelling risk-reward trade-off 
US 10-year government bond yield and previous highs

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

At current levels, the US 10-year government bond yield 
offers investors compelling risk-reward trade off

year Developed Market government bonds from shifts in 
their yields; grey dotted lines roughly denote current spot 
yield levels

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 28 September 2026

Investor positioning adjustment: In the very near-term, the 
US 10 -year bond yield has room to move lower as hedge 
funds using systematic strategies (CTAs) are extremely short 
US government bonds, raising the chance of short -covering 
if upcoming jobs and inflation data miss estimates. 
A shallow Fed hiking cycle  
We expect the shallowest Fed hiking cycle in almost 
three decades. This should enable the US 10-year yield to 
ease towards 4.75 -5.0% over a 12 -month period. Following 
this month’s increase, we forecast two further 25bp hikes by 
June 2027, followed by a cut in H2 2027. Markets price at 
least three more hikes by June and a strong probability of a 
fourth by September. Our projected 75bp s of total hikes this 
cycle compares with 525bps of hikes in 2022–23, 225bps in 
2015–18, 425bps in 2004–06 and 175bp in 1999–2000. The 
last smaller mid-cycle move was a single 25bps hike in 1997. 
Why we expect a more cautious Fed 
The latest hike restored the Fed’s credibility after renewed 
oil-price pressures, elevated inflation and a balanced labour 
market prompted a unanimous policy vote. However, the 
following factors argue against aggressive tightening: 
Supply-driven inflation: Energy costs and AI-infrastructure 
constraints, rather than excess demand, are driving prices. 
Higher rates cannot repair supply and may weaken growth. 
Labour is no longer the main inflation source:  Labour 
supply and demand are better balanced, wage growth has 
moderated and unemployment is near full employment. AI 
further weakens the traditional Phillips Curve relationship 
between unemployment and inflation. 
Disinflation should resume:  Energy pressures may fade 
after the US mid-term elections, while the one-off inflationary 
effects of tariffs should diminish after 2025–26. 
AI raises the neutral rate , but dampens inflation:  
Investment and productivity may lift the neutral rate ( r*), the 
rate consistent with stable inflation and full employment, 
while efficiency gains limit wage-price spirals. 
Markets are already tightening  financial conditions : 
Higher bond yields have raised borrowing costs and 
restrained activity, doing part of the Fed’s work. 
The cycle began from a higher base:  The Fed funds rate 
started at 3.75%, versus 0.25% in the previous two cycles 
and 1% before that, reducing additional tightening required. 
Monetary transmission has weakened:  Wealthier 
households drive more spending but are less rate -sensitive, 
while lower -income consumers are already constrained. 
Further hikes may , therefore, curb demand less effectively 
while causing disproportionate harm to low-income families. 
 We expect a shallower Fed rate hiking cycle compared 
with market expectations

tariffs, long-term inflation expectations remain subdued

### Page 3

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 28 September 2026

Small business conditions are deteriorating:  Higher 
financing and energy costs are weakening sentiment, as 
reflected in the latest Beige Book . The renewed 
underperformance of US small capitalisation (S&P 600) and 
mid capitalisation (S&P 400) equity indices  versus the large 
cap S&P 500 index over the past couple of months also show 
rising pressure on small businesses. 
Fiscal and financial -stability risks are rising:  Aggressive 
Fed tightening would increase US debt-servicing costs as the 
US Treasury seeks cheaper funding through shorter-maturity 
issuance, while potentially amplifying financial-system stress. 
Upside risk to bond yields 
Our view of lower bond yields carries the following risks: 
Persistent inflation:  Repeated supply shocks, strong AI -
related investment demand and tighter immigration controls 
could keep inflation above target and force steeper Fed rate 
hikes. With unemployment near the Fed’s estimate of full 
employment, a sharp drop in labour supply could intensify 
wage pressures and destabilise inflation expectations . We 
note that long-term expectations remain contained for now. 
Geopolitical escalation:  An intensification of geopolitical 
conflicts after the mid -term elections could lift energy prices 
and inflation. However, a Democrat -controlled or divided 
Congress may constrain US foreign-policy initiatives through 
tighter budgets, while Republicans could turn less supportive 
as attention shifts to the 2028 presidential race. 
Higher neutral rate: AI investments could raise the neutral 
rate (r*), turning current monetary policy settings 
accommodative. This could necessitate further rate hikes. 
However, the Fed has barely raised its nominal neutral rate 
estimate, lifting it to 3.2% in September from 3.0% a year ago. 
Bottomline: The outlook for rates remains geopolitics 
dependent, with all Fed meetings live . However, based on 
our core scenario, we see the US 10-year yield settling lower 
in the 4.75-5.0% in 6-12 months. A shallow Fed hiking cycle 
due to expected disinflation offers investors a reasonably 
attractive risk-reward in US 10-year government bonds. 
US 30-year government bonds , on the other hand, may 
continue to see elevated yields as the US fiscal deficit is 
unlikely to have improved next year, and may look much 
worse. As a result, “term premium” may keep the ultra -long 
end elevated. Short of some unorthodox policy to artifici ally 
cap them lower, the 30-year yield is likely to continue to drift 
higher towards the 5.5-5.75% range into H2 2027. 
— Jonathan Liang, CIO for Fixed Income and FX   
Rajat Bhattacharya, Senior Investment Strategist 
Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

tariff effects on inflation to fade over 12 months, 
offsetting AI-driven effects on bond yields

## Document 295: Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

September 2026: Marked improvement in
services business confidence
September 2026: Business climate in the
manufacturing sector comfortably in the
expansion zone for the 8th month in a row
September 2026: Strengthening inflationary
pressures
September 2026: Slight further increase in
supply tensions
September 2026: First decrease in 5 months of
household confidence
September 2026: Marked increase in inflation,
to a 3-year high
Eurozone: Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer

confidence falls back
02/10/2026 3
We have selected two inflation measures (with and without energy) and six survey indicators: 1/ & 2/ business confidence, as
measured by the PMIs in the manufacturing and services sectors; 3/ & 4/ input and output prices components of the
composite PMI (in order to identify direct inflationary pressures); 5/ the suppliers’ delivery times component of the
manufacturing PMI (a direct indicator of possible supply difficulties and supply-demand imbalance, and therefore, indirectly,

of inflationary pressures in the making); and 6/ consumer confidence (measured by the “assessment of financial situation in
the next 12 months” in order to capture the impact of inflation on purchasing power). The trends in each of these indicators
are observed relative to month m=0, corresponding to the start of the conflict. Each line does not represent the level of the
indicator, but its cumulative variation compared to month m=0.
The overall picture from the confidence surveys available for September is globally positive. According to the PMIs, the

business climate in services has indeed seen a clear improvement, and that in the manufacturing sector remains comfortably
in expansion territory for the 8 th month in a row. However, consumer confidence has recorded its first decline in 5 months,
likely driven by rising inflation and concerns over purchasing power. News on the inflation front is negative. PMI surveys
report a strengthening of inflationary pressures: this trend is not a surprise given the tensions in energy prices, but it is

nonetheless significant. As for inflation itself, it continues to rise, more markedly in September: +0.6 percentage points, to
3.8% year-on-year (Eurostat flash estimate), of which 0.4 pp du to the higher energy contribution. Inflation excluding energy
increased as well, by 0.2 pp to 2.3% (of which 0.1 pp due to the rise in food inflation).
Source: S&P Global, Eurostat, European Commission, Macrobond, BNP Paribas

## Document 319: India_ Degrees of rupee defence (2026-10-16)

Source PDF: all report pdf/India_ Degrees of rupee defence (2026-10-16).pdf
Report source: DBS
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

• The RBI has adopted a multi -

• Parallels with 2013 exist, 
although the circumstances 
differ.

elsewhere.

• Scope of further rate 
increases is high.  Not a 
panacea but a necessary step.  
• If FX depreciation pressures 
return, a  larger scale of hike 
or inter-meeting move might 
also be considered.

### Page 2

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

Editorial 
Will earnings resolve the bond-equity split?  
Strategy summary:  Bond and equity market volatility has 
sharply diverged. Elevated bond volatility and political risks in 
the US and Europe warrant near-term caution, prompting us to 
lock in tactical gains in semiconductors and Taiwan equities. 
Our quantitative stock-bond model turned negative for the first 
time since March 2025, signalling near-term equity volatility . 
We stay invested in a diversified allocation  slightly overweight 
equities and prefer to navigate the upcoming results and macro 
events before adding high-beta exposure within equities. 
Consensus estimates point to a c. 31% y/y rise in S&P500 Q3 
earnings, driven by technology -sector earnings growth of c. 
67%. Earnings have beaten lofty estimates in H1 as the AI 
build-out exceeds expectations. This favours tech -led equities 
in the medium term. In Europe, French political and fiscal risks 
cloud the EUR outlook, but regional bank equities remain 
attractive on robust profitability, capital strength and valuations. 
Divergence between equities and bonds.  Equity markets 
signal calm, with the S&P 500 index near record highs and its 
volatility index (VIX) low at 15, while the US government bond 
volatility index (MOVE) is close to its highest since March. Bond 
stress reflects two-decade-high long yields amid strong growth, 
oil close to USD 100/bbl fuelling near -term inflation, fiscal 
uncertainty and crowded investor short positioning vulnerable 
to abrupt unwinds. High bond volatility could spill into equities. 
Our quantitative stock-bond model turns negative. For the 
first time since March 2025, the model score has turned 
negative, driven by deteriorating equity market breadth and 
above-average risk aversion. History shows global equity 
volatility rises most in the month after the score dips to -1. 
However, equities still outperformed bonds over the following 
1-12 months, on average, despite higher market risk. 
Taking some chips off the table; locking in gains:  Rising 
bond volatility, the negative stock -bond model signal, weak 
equity breadth and approaching US mid-term elections warrant 
near-term caution. US small -caps remain about 9% below 
August’s all-time highs even as large-caps reach new records. 
The macro uncertainty prompted us to lock in gains in 
semiconductor and Taiwan equity opportunistic ideas. 
Strong Q3 earnings season likely to overcome near -term 
challenges. Strong US and Asian earnings should ultimately 
resolve the equity -bond divergence in favour of stocks. 
Consensus S&P 500 Q3 earnings growth has risen to 30 .6% 
y/y, while technology is tracking 66.5%, driven by the AI 
infrastructure buildout. AI investment plans, monetisation and 
funding needs are key metrics to watch. Record memory -chip 
profits and higher long -term targets from AI leaders support 
staying long technology-led risk assets and using near -term 
volatility to scale into preferred sectors. Also, US equities have 
historically performed strongly in the 12 months after mid -term 
elections, particularly when the opposition regains control of the 
House and the Senate – as seems increasingly likely. 
Medium-term constructive on equities. Beyond Q3 earnings, 
we expect US earnings to rise 15-20% next year. Almost half of 
the estimated USD 420 per share of S&P500 earnings in 2027 
should come from tech-related sectors: information technology, 
communication services and consumer discretionary. We also 
favour US, Europe and Japan financial sectors as solid growth 
and higher rates lift lending income, while the AI buildout boosts 
advisory fees from IPOs and M&A s. However, a nother 30-
50bps rise in long-term bond yields , without earnings growth 
upgrades, could be a  headwind for equities.  Next week’s US 
inflation data for September is the next catalyst for bond yields. 
French uncertainty challenges near -term EUR view; 
continue to see opportunities in Euro area bank equities.  
Rising French political and fiscal risks have largely remained 
confined to France, with its bond yield spread versus German 
peers surging to the widest since the 2011-12 Euro area crisis. 
Nevertheless, EUR/USD has fallen around 4% since mid -
August, and our three-month forecast of 1.15 is under review. 
Tighter financial conditions and political uncertainty in France 
and Spain raises the risk of a n ECB rate hold. Euro area bank 
equities remain attractive on profitability, capital strength and 
valuations, subject to sovereign-spread and funding-cost risks.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

Top client questions 
  What are the market implications of France's political and 
fiscal uncertainty?   
Our view: France’s political and fiscal uncertainty remain near-
term headwinds for European assets. We are placing our three-
month EUR/USD forecast (1.15) under review for a potential 
downward adjustment, while retaining our 12-month forecast at 
1.18 as broader Euro  area spillovers remain limited. Our base 
case remains one more ECB hike in December followed by a 
prolonged pause, although tighter financial conditions increase 
the risk of a delay. For equities, we retain Euro area banks as 
an Opportunistic idea, supported by strong profitability, capital 
buffers and reasonable valuations. For bank bonds, we prefer a 
subordinated capital structure to senior debt. 
Rationale: France’s 10-year government bond yield has risen amid 
renewed political and fiscal concerns. This has tightened financial 
conditions and added to the near-term EUR risk premium. Together 
with elevated US yields, this raises downside risks to our three-
month EUR/USD view. However, the stress has not yet developed 
into broad Euro area contagion, and recent expectations of progress 
on France’s 2027 budget have provided some relief. Broader Euro  
area growth and labour  market fundamentals also remain relatively 
resilient, supporting our unchanged 12-month EUR/USD view. 
For the ECB, higher sovereign yields are already delivering some 
monetary tightening. Markets have consequently pared expectations 
of additional ECB hikes. We continue to expect one more hike in 
December, given elevated near -term inflation, but persistent fiscal 
or political stress in France and Spain could delay further tightening. 
For Euro area banks, wider sovereign spreads  are likely to  raise 
funding costs and pressure bond portfolios, while fewer ECB hikes 
would moderate further gains in net interest income. Nevertheless, 
profitability remains strong, capital buffers are healthy and price-
to-book valuations remain reasonable, with returns on equity also 
improving. We, therefore, maintain Euro area banks as an 
Opportunistic idea , while monitoring sovereign spreads, funding 
costs and any deterioration in loan-loss provisions.  
On the bond side, European bank junior subordinated bond prices 
fell by an average of 3-5 cash points before rebounding, with French 
bank subordinated bonds underperforming those of other European 
peers. As fundamentals remain strong, we view pullbacks as 
opportunities to add and maintain our preference for a subordinated 
capital structure over senior debt for European bank bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist  
 Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the likelihood of elevated long -end government 
bond yields evolving into a systemic risk for the financial sector 
and public finances? 
Our view: We believe s ystemic risk remains a tail risk rather 
than a base case in the near term. The probability would rise 
materially if rollover stress, foreign demand withdrawal and 
private credit deterioration occur in combination. 
Rationale: Higher bond yields worsen fiscal deficits and drive higher 
issuance requirements, exerting further upward pressure on yields. 
The resulting tighter financial conditions weigh on economic growth. 
This feedback loop is most acute in Developed Markets (DMs) where 
debt burdens are already elevated. For instance, projected 2026 
debt-to-GDP ratios stand at 204% for Japan, 138% for Italy, 126% 
for the US, 118% for France and 104% for the UK. Countries with 
shorter average debt maturities face the sharpest near-term 
repricing risk  as maturing obligations are refinanced at current 
rates. Meanwhile, evidence suggests that  a positive equity-bond 
correlation increases the sensitivity of yields to bond supply.  
Taking the US as an example, there has been no buyers’ strike on 
US government bonds yet , though foreign demand has softened, 
narrowing the margin of safety. The US government has shifted 
towards shorter-dated issuance to manage funding costs (reducing 
near-term expenses, but concentrating rollover risk). Economic 
resilience remains evident, underpinned by corporate confidence 
in AI -driven investment . The key question is whether this 
expansion can persist long enough to stabilise the fiscal trajectory. 
Within the financial sector, corporate balance sheets  remain 
strong, while High-Yield (HY) bond and leveraged loan default rates 
remain contained, though this reflects the lag between rate rises and 
credit deterioration, not immunity. Following the failure of the Silicon 
Valley Bank, liquidity discipline among banks has improved, but 
duration mismatches persist.  
In private credit, default rates are trending upward while the asset 
class has come under continued scrutiny , with negative headlines 
and a rise in redemptions trigger ing fund gating  at some  private 
credit managers since Q 1 2026. Hence, continued monitoring of 
default rates, redemptions and potential stress events in private 
credit remains essential . That said, higher rates could buoy 
medium-term returns and distribution yields in the asset class , 
as the majority of direct lending loans have a floating-rate structure. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

Standard Chartered Bank

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for the US Q3 2026 earnings season, 
especially for the banking sector? 
Our view: We expect another quarter of robust US earnings 
growth to support our Overweight stance on US equities. We 
see m ajor US banks, which begin reporting next week, also 
delivering healthy results, reinforcing our Overweight view on  
the US financial sector. 
Rationale: Consensus forecasts S&P500 Q3 earnings growth  of 
30.6% y/y, up from 27.6% at the start of July. Energy is expected to 
post the strongest growth at 114.7%, supported by elevated oil 
prices amid ongoing geopolitical tensions. Information technology 
(66.5%) and communication services (49.0%) remain key 
earnings drivers, benefiting from sustained AI -related demand and 
investment. The broader outlook remains supportive, with full-year 
earnings growth projected at 35.8% in 2026 and 15.6% in 2027. 
For banks, the recent underperformance amid concerns over higher 
long-term bond yields has, in our view, improved the forward-looking 
risk-reward profile.  Over the medium term, strengthening loan 
growth and resilient credit quality should continue to support solid 
fundamentals. Capital markets activity also remains constructive, 
with industry leaders well -positioned to benefit from their strong 
investment banking and trading franchises. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst 
  How could upcoming semiconductor manufacturing and 
equipment earnings reshape the AI capex outlook? 
Our view: We maintain our AI capex outlook as robust demand 
and ongoing supply constraints support a multi-year expansion 
cycle. In the near term, however, we take a more prudent stance 
on semiconductors amid an expected rise in equity volatility. 
Rationale: Recent memory industry guidance points to sustained 
investment in advanced chip manufacturing , with customer 
commitments extending through 2031. Upcoming earnings should 
provide visibility into capacity expansion plans. This supports our AI 
capex outlook, which we will monitor through the earnings season.  
That said, we expect equity volatility to rise in the near term amid 
elevated long-term yields and macroeconomic uncertainty. Recent 
media reports on AI lab revenues  have also led to doubts over 
monetisation and the ability to sustain elevated capex spending. We 
thus took profit on our Global Semiconductors (8.1% return from 9-
Sep-26 to 2-Oct-26) and MSCI Taiwan (10.0% return from 9-Jul-26 to 
2-Oct-26) Opportunistic ideas following strong performance. This does 
not reflect a deterioration in fundamentals, but rather a need to be 
prudent on high-beta exposure. We remain constructive on the AI 
theme over the long term. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 9 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  USD/CHF has rebounded sharply over the past two 
months. What are the key factors behind the move, and do you 
see the currency pair continuing to move higher? 
Our view:  USD/CHF tested 0.84 amid strong momentum from 
the USD, but we see the pair remain ing supported near -term 
before gradually moving lower towards 0.82 in three months. 
Rationale: The Fed’s relatively high policy rate continues to support 
USD carry demand , while the Swiss National Bank maintains its 
policy rate at 0%. In the near term, however, the Swiss franc (CHF) 
could benefit from increased safe-haven demand amid mounting 
fiscal concerns in France  and political uncertainty in Spain , 
particularly if European risk premia continue to rise. Beyond these 
cyclical factors, Switzerland’s substantial net foreign asset position, 
persistent current account surpluses and low public debt burden 
continue to underpin the CHF’s structural strength . Over the 
medium term, we expect the USD’s rate advantage to gradually 
diminish as the Fed approaches the end of its tightening cycle, while 
the CHF’s defensive characteristics should remain supportive.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  Do you see further upside for USD/JPY after recent dovish 
comments from BoJ officials and latest bond auctions? 
Our view: We see some near-term upside risk to USD/JPY from 
elevated US  yields, but retain our medium -term constructive 
JPY view. USD/JPY remains below 159, suggesting markets are 
mindful of intervention risk as the pair approaches 160. We 
continue to expect further BoJ normalisation. 
Rationale: Recent BoJ communication appears less dovish than the 
market narrative had suggested. BoJ Governor Ueda continues to 
emphasise the need to anchor underlying inflation around 2%  
and gradually reduce monetary accommodation, supporting our view 
that the tightening cycle is not yet over. An October pause would 
reflect a more measured pace of policy normalisation  rather than a 
change in the BoJ’s policy direction. 
Recent Japanese government bond (JGB) auctions have shown 
resilient demand despite higher yields.  At the latest 10 -year JGB 
auction, the bid -to-cover ratio improved  to 3.76x from 3.29x 
previously, while the auction tail (spread between highest 
accepted yield and issued yield) narrowed to 0.2bps from 1.6bps. 
We see this as indicative of an orderly, functioning market rather than 
a dovish BoJ signal . Intervention concerns around  the 160 level 
should help limit near-term USD/JPY upside.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

《消費量能未見失速訊號》
• 8月總體市場消費數據偏保守，
然維持正向表現
1
資產 4Q26 看法 投資展望
核心消費 中立 從已公告的核心消費個股展望，本行預期今年上下半年營收成長48：52
的溫和成長預期，同時零售商備貨行為與終端消費需求仍支持到貨量的
基本盤，但消費者實際支出意願可能受到油價／利率上升抑制，導致平
均單價、毛利率受到一定壓縮，尤其在低、中所得族群及易受油價影響
的旅遊／實體零售類別。4Q26預計呈「量能可望維持、獲利偏弱」格局
資料來源：美國銀行2026/9/15，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/18。

• 消費量能未見失速訊號 P.1
• 「K型」分化持續收斂，低所得消
費動能並非來自舉債 P.2
• 重塑消費環境與消費類股估值基礎 P.3
• 4Q26傳統銷售旺季，考驗獲利 P.4
消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/9/21
本行長期追蹤美國銀行信用卡消費統計數據(Consumer
checkpoimt，月報) 顯示，8月消費動能自7月疲弱中明顯
反彈。從美國銀行發行的信用卡、簽帳卡合計消費YoY成
長率已連續三個月降溫（6月6.3%→7月5.0%→8月4.5%）
剔除油品後同步走緩（5.6%→4.3%→3.7%），主因6月世
界盃與線上促銷時序錯位帶來的高基期效應消退。惟季調
後MoM動能出現反轉，7月一度下滑（-0.2%），8月明顯
回升（+0.9%）。商務部數據同步佐證：8月零售銷售季調
後月增1.2%，優於市場預期0.7%，成長動能來自線上零售、
加油站（價格推升）及餐飲、電子產品等類別。

• 中低所得族群而言，目前的消

基礎
2
《「K型」分化持續收斂，低所得消費並非來自舉債》
8 月非必需消費 YoY成長 ， 高所得 （ 5.9% ） 與低所得
（5.7%）差距僅0.2個百分點 ，為2024年1月以來最小；
中所得 （5.1%）略為落後 。低所得族群稅後工資成長已
連續第二個月領先其他所得層。
另紐約聯準銀行資料顯示 ，2026年第二季家庭總負債為

18.771兆美元 ，季減130億美元 ，年增3,830億美元；信
用卡餘額季增210億美元、年增540億美元至1.263兆美元，
負債溫和成長，並非急遽加槓桿。嚴重違約（90天以上）
的 年 化 轉 出 率 方 面， 信 用 卡 由 去 年 同 期6.93% 微升至
6.97%。這與美銀 Checkpoint 揭露的另一項正面訊號互
相呼應：所有所得層級「每月全額繳清信用卡帳單」的家
庭比例都在上升，僅繳最低應繳金額的比例雖也上升但幅
度較小。綜合來看，原先市場擔憂的低所得者會藉由舉債
消費，但目前數據並不支持這樣的看法 ，等於是提供K型
收斂假說的有力證據
《重塑消費環境與消費類股估值基礎》
9月上旬發生兩項關鍵變化 ，根本性重塑消費環境與消費
類股估值基礎：聯準會於 9/16啟動三年多來首次升息 （1
碼至3.75%-4.00%），新任主席沃許（Kevin Warsh）強
調通膨仍偏高 ，點陣顯示年內可能再加 1碼；同時密西根
大學消費者信心指數9月初值急跌至47.8，創自1978年以

來次低。
資料來源：美國銀行 2026/9/15，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/18

《4Q26傳統銷售旺季Bottom-Up需求強弱度分析》

由於NRF尚未發布2026年假期旺季（11-12月）專屬銷售
預測（歷年慣例於10-11月發布），且目前可用最新官方
預測為發生在中東戰爭之前。因此改採bottom-up方式，
以供需鏈前置指標、零售商財測、消費者信心與成本端壓
力四大面向交叉驗證。
➢ NRF Global Port Tracker預估9月將為全年主要貨櫃港
口進口量最高的月份，異於往年7-8月峰期的季節性規
律，顯示零售商對4Q旺季需求仍持樂觀，提前備貨以
應對可能的關稅／運輸不確定性。
➢ 零售商財測訊號分化，Target（同比銷售+3.8%，營收
+5.3%，全年EPS指引上調至$9.90-10.90）與Best
Buy（同比銷售+4.1%，全年指引上調至營收$42.3-
42.8bn，同店銷售1.9-3%）均上調全年指引。
資料來源：密西根大學2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/18

零售客流量表

業績

off-price（折扣）業者同店成

• 4Q26傳統旺季需求呈現「量能
可望維持、獲利品質面臨分化
壓力」的中性格局
4
，反映電子／電腦、家居、美妝、新品類需求扎實。相較
之下，Walmart雖也上調全年指引 ，但Q3單季指引轉軟
（EPS $0.62-0.64，同店銷售3.0-3.75%），股價在公布
後重挫9%——市場解讀為Q2超預期主要來自一次性關稅
退款效應（約750個基點的毛利貢獻 ），而非需求結構性
改善 ，該退款已大幅讓利投入價格競爭 （單季推出超過

1.1萬項降價），意味著Q2的優異表現部分係提前透支Q3。
同時電商與數位通路持續為成長引擎： Walmart全球電商
+23% 、 Target數位同店 +8.7% 、 Home Depot 線上
+11%（連續五季雙位數成長 ）、Lowe's線上+15.7%，
顯示全通路投資持續帶動業績。
➢ 消費者「精打細算」特徵明顯：off-price（折扣）業態
如TJX、Ross Stores同店成長強勁（分別+4%、
+10%），反映消費者尋求高性價比商品的行為持續強
化。
➢ 關稅退款效應將於Q3起逐漸消退，市場焦點將轉向排
除一次性利益後的核心毛利率與同店銷售動能是否可持
續。
➢ 燃油與運費成本為共同逆風：各公司財報及法說會均提
及油價、運輸成本上升對下半年毛利率的壓力。
結論：4Q26傳統旺季需求呈現「量能可望維持、獲利
品質面臨分化壓力」的中性格局，與兩個月前「消費持續
熱絡」的單一樂觀敘事相比，風險報酬比已明顯向下傾斜
(亦即產業獲利成長動能趨緩)，整體消費的評價面難有向
上提升的動力來源。

非核心消費產業投資攻略
出 刊 日 : 2026/10/13

• 非核心消費板塊獲利前景近
期蒙上陰霾。首先，郵輪業
雖受惠於旅遊需求韌性而上
調 2026 財年預測，但高利
率與高油價恐使後市持續承
壓；其次，運動鞋服產業受
到大中華區市場衰退與新興
小品牌崛起雙重打擊，面臨
營收與獲利雙雙下滑的困境
，短期內組織重整效益恐難
顯現。再者，在即將迎來的
財報季前夕，市場共識普遍
預期 3Q26 的增長將陷入停
滯。
《市場共識預期3Q26非核心消費板塊成長停滯》
美3Q26財報季將陸續揭曉 ，目前市場共識預期標
普500指數各產業板塊第三季的獲利成長存在較大
差異，其中資訊科技和能源板塊將推動指數整體獲
利成長，預期非核心消費板塊整體每股盈餘將不會
成長。另外，零星個股捎來截至 8月底的季度業績
也顯示好壞參半的結果。
《郵輪旅遊需求仍具韌性，燃油成本仍是潛在風險》
嘉年華郵輪 (CCL)3Q26財年(截至8月底)營收年增
3.5%，每股盈餘持平 ，淨收益率超預期 ，主要貢
獻來自船上及其它收入，然因燃料成本大增36%，
完全抵銷了營運效率與成本控制的效益 ，導致調整

後EBITDA持平。管理層上調2026年全年獲利展望 ，
且公司表示 2027年訂位量及訂位價格均創下歷史
新高，目前已售出約半數 2027年艙位 ，顯示旅遊
需求仍具韌性。但市場對其未來半年的獲利預期偏
共識預期整體非核心消費3Q26盈餘0成長
標普500 3Q26財報自下而上市場共識預估

保守，主要是9 月以來居高不下的利率與一路攀升
的油價，或將直接推高其利息負擔與燃油開銷。由
於CCL採取未對沖燃油的採購策略，油價走高可能
轉化為每股盈餘的實質逆風，使其獲利能力在未來
幾季面臨嚴峻考驗。
《運動鞋服Nike轉型成效慢，獲利展望不佳》
Nike1Q27財年(2026/6~2026/8)營收與每股盈餘
皆衰退。運動休閒系列、Jordan品牌和大中華區市
場下滑 ，成為主要逆風 。管理層預期 2027財年營
收將出現高個位數下滑，提出的每股盈餘財測也低
於市場預期 。即便Nike推出名為 Pace營運重整計
畫，但效益明顯無法在短期內看到 。其中一個原因
是Nike的市場主導地位已不如前，小型品牌如今已

具備規模化營運能力，當消費者行為已發生結構性
轉變時(品牌忠誠度降低與多品牌嘗試 )，如何收復
市佔率就成為一項艱巨且持久的挑戰。
《2026 美年底消費總額預期創新高 ，逾半增長靠
「通膨」，電商與生成式AI成雙引擎》
近期已有多家研調機構針對美國2026年年底消費
旺季出具預測報告。整體來看，各大機構預測的銷
售總額皆創下歷史新高 ，但其背後的共同隱憂是
「通膨」，直言超過一半的名目增長率是由物價上
漲驅動。這使得消費者普遍展現「價格敏感、主動
尋找折扣、切換品牌以省錢」的價值導向行為。電
商增速(網購)依然大幅碾壓實體零售。所有機構也
都點出生成式AI在2026年成為主流購物工具。
資料來源：Mastercard， 2026/9/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/13

資料來源：Mastercard/Deloitte/Adobe， 2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/13
機構預測2026年底消費旺季
各家機構重點分述如下
Mastercard ： 預 期 假日消費成長近半因物價上漲 ，
今年主因能源與AI建設成本增加，去年則受關稅影
響。鑑於柴油價格持續高企可能推高零售商的運輸
成本，並對年底的整體消費價格構成上行壓力，因
此值得密切關注。高收入家庭因財富成長、增加自
由支配支出，成為近年消費成長的重要引領者， 然
而，受益於勞動市場穩定、薪資成長與健康資產負
債表，各收入群體亦持續穩定貢獻，消費仍由廣大
家庭共同支撐。
Adobe：預計今年假期季節將推出極具競爭力的折

扣，雖然消費者歷來都熱衷於利用節日促銷活動購
買禮物或添加最新電子產品，但許多人現在更傾向
於利用折扣囤積生活必需品。另，今年假期季節美
國零售網站的人工智慧流 量 將年增130%。預計感
恩節的增幅最大(年增159%)，其次是黑色星期五(
年成長 95%)和網路星期一(年增88%)。
德勤(Deloitte) ： 消費者仍然重視為親朋好友營造
特別的節日氛圍，同時也在精打細算地消費。為了
更好地利用每一分錢，觀察到各個收入階層的消費
者都在追求性價比，例如在不同品牌和零售商之間
切換，以及利用促銷活動來控制支出。

中国移动互联网：扩张接近尾声，整体进入存量竞争
移动互联网早期行业扩张渐近尾声，整体规模增长趋缓，用户时长稳步提升，行业大盘进入存量竞争阶段
中国移动互联网早期通过信息、消费及服务线上化创造新增需求的增量红利期基本结束，互联网整体流量大盘保持稳定，用户规模增长趋缓，使用时长温和提升。22

年至26年8月，月用户规模与月总有效时间整体均小幅增长，移动互联网行业已进入低速增长阶段，互联网发展阶段进一步由信息获取、通讯社交走向内容消费和娱乐
互动为主的内容消费时代，平台间竞争更多体现为存量用户和使用时长的争夺。
来源：艾瑞咨询UserTracker多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制。

### Page 5

5©2026.9 iResearch Inc.                                                                                                                         www.iresearch.com.cn
头部集团用户覆盖趋顶，竞争重心转向时长
字节系实现跨越式追赶，与腾讯系、阿里系用户覆盖齐触上限，头部集团竞争重心转向注意力时长
2026年8月，腾讯系、阿里系、字节系月用户规模（以月独立设备数衡量）分别达到14.4亿、14.0亿和13.6亿，均已超过全网月用户规模的90%，头部集团用户覆盖
逐步趋近上限。过去四年，字节系月用户规模增长超过3亿，快速追赶至与腾讯系、阿里系同一量级，日用户规模（以当月日均独立设备数衡量）从5.9亿攀升至8.3亿，
超越阿里系，位居行业第二；随着用户规模增长空间收窄，依靠新增用户拉动增长的难度提升，头部集团竞争逐步由用户规模转向用户注意力与使用时长，时长份额

和使用深度成为决定竞争优势的关键，用户时长不仅代表平台的粘性与可持续留存能力，更直接决定了广告曝光总量、内容消费频次与交易转化规模的基础大盘。
来源：艾瑞咨询 UserTracker 多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制。

APP总时长：字节系持续提升份额，已超越腾讯系
用户注意力高度集中于头部APP，字节系凭短视频等泛娱乐内容生态实现对腾讯系时长超越并持续拉开差距
• Top 50 APP长期承载全网九成以上用户有效时长，时长已成为头部平台竞争的核心资源。
• 22年至26年8月，字节系在Top 50 APP中的时长份额持续提升，并于2025年超越腾讯系成为时长份额最高集团，26年差距进一步拉大。
• 字节系份额提升，主要得益于抖音、红果免费短剧、番茄小说、汽水音乐等产品在短视频、短剧、在线阅读、在线音乐等全场景内容协同矩阵的持续扩张，实现对
用户碎片化与深度时间的无缝锁定，扩大用户使用场景，提升用户注意力覆盖。
• 以2026年8月Top 50 APP各集团单月时长统计口径为例，在商业化程度最高的内容类与电商类时长中，字节系的市场份额已超50%。
来源：艾瑞咨询UserTracker多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制。

注：时长占比=各集团所属TOP50APP当月总时长÷TOP50APP当月总时长，为保证集团份额可比性，样本按当期Top50口径选取，以下份额均为当期Top50
样本份额。字节系APP包括抖音、抖音火山版、今日头条、番茄免费小说、西瓜视频、红果免费短剧、番茄畅听、豆包等；腾讯系APP包括微信、QQ、腾讯
新闻、腾讯视频、王者荣耀、QQ浏览器、QQ音乐、酷狗音乐等；阿里系APP包括淘宝、高德地图、优酷视频等。

7©2026.9 iResearch Inc.                                                                                                                         www.iresearch.com.cn
用户时长：主要集团用户单日时长对比
字节系用户使用深度持续提升，单日时长领先优势不断扩大
• 26年8月，字节系用户单机单日有效时长升达1.9小时，较22年6月增加0.6小时，在23年超越腾讯后差距持续拉大，用户使用深度显著高于其他头部集团；
• 相比APP总时长份额，字节系在用户使用深度上的优势凸显，26年8月字节系用户单日时长达腾讯系的近1.5倍，表现出极强的单用户粘性与时间锁定能力；
• 阿里系应用承接具体需求用户使用时长稳定且有限，腾讯系用户使用时长小幅下降；内容消费时代下，用户时间结构呈现由通讯社交进一步向内容娱乐深化倾斜趋
势，字节系依托短视频等多维泛娱乐内容生态，不断吸纳消化这一结构性转移流量，用户使用深度持续提升。

来源：艾瑞咨询 UserTracker 多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制。

典型行业APP时长
各头部集团在核心行业分别占据主导地位，字节系在泛娱乐等高时长占比领域持续抢占用户时间覆盖
字节系领跑短视频等核心内容赛道，腾讯系稳固通信社交基本盘，阿里系保持电子商务领域优势。同时，字节系依托抖音主站拓展电商、本地生活消费场景，其电商
规模快速接近阿里系；本地生活（到店）亦逐步接近美团，业务边界持续拓宽；字节系生态实现高时长占比场景时长领先，以短视频为超级流量入口，新闻资讯、短
剧、阅读等泛娱乐内容高时长占比领域领跑，多APP矩阵承接用户的全方位需求，拓宽对用户生命周期的占据范围。
来源：艾瑞咨询UserTracker 多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制。

### Page 9

9©2026.9 iResearch Inc.                                                                                                                         www.iresearch.com.cn
时长决定格局，泛娱乐内容入口以时长构建竞争壁垒
头部集团用户覆盖见顶，存量竞争转向对用户时长的争夺，泛娱乐型入口凭借多元内容APP占据用户注意力
互联网进入存量竞争，头部平台之间的竞争转向对存量用户注意力与停留时长的争夺。泛娱乐内容、社交、交易/工具三类入口的用户时长呈现明显分化：
①以字节系为代表的泛娱乐内容型入口，凭借短视频、短剧、在线阅读等多类型的高频内容持续抢占用户注意力，时长份额持续上升；
②社交型入口凭借通讯社交覆盖大多数用户，时长基数大，但在内容类型丰富度方面相对不足，对用户注意力吸引有限，时长份额有所回落；
③交易/工具型入口的用户进入目的明确，完成后即离开，停留时长有限。用户时长正向娱乐内容集中，拥有多样化娱乐内容生态的平台，持续吸纳流量与用户时长。

来源：艾瑞咨询UserTracker多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制。
①泛娱乐内容型入口：占据注意力，时长份额持续上升 ②社交型入口：覆盖最广，时长份额有所回落 ③交易/工具型入口：目的明确，停留时长有限

• 通讯社交为第二大时长行业（20.2%），腾讯系占其中
79.8%，用户覆盖率为各集团最高
• 社交型APP属于目的驱动用户停留，以完成沟通与任务为主
• 在线音乐行业，腾讯系仍保持优势；但短视频等高频内容的

时长增量主要流向内容型入口，这使得腾讯系与字节系的时
长差距拉大
约12%
阿里系、百度系、美团时长份额合计
（交易与工具型入口）
• 阿里系在网络购物（52.8%）、美团在本地生活（84.3%）
仍占据核心入口，用户覆盖率同样超过九成
• 用户带着需求进入，比价下单或完成搜索后离开，单次停留
短，时长占比低
• 进入意图明确使其单次流量价值高，但可供售卖的注意力总
量有限
用户规模决定覆盖的广度，使用时长决定注意力的深度；时长向娱乐内容集中，是当前流量格局形成的直接原因
互联网发展阶段：
竞争重心：
信息获取时代
抢占信息入口
交易与社交时代
获取用户规模
内容消费时代
争夺用户时间
内容消费时代，用户时间结构由通讯社交等逐步向内容娱乐倾斜，短视
频类高注意力内容型平台成为互联网主要时间入口。

### Page 10

10©2026.9 iResearch Inc.                                                                                                                         www.iresearch.com.cn
1.3
1.9
字节系产品矩阵、算法推荐和交叉导流形成流量内循环
以抖音为流量中枢构建矩阵，通过产品交叉导流实现流量内循环，依托多元内容驱动注意力沉淀与时长集中
字节系以抖音为流量中枢，搭建覆盖多场景的产品矩阵，并通过抖音向矩阵产品分流，各产品用户与抖音的重合率普遍超过90% 。矩阵产品承接差异化需求后，产出
内容素材并回流抖音，同时矩阵产品间也基于产出内容进行交叉导流，综合形成矩阵内流量的循环。这一流量内循环机制，驱动字节系在月用户规模、月使用时长份
额占比以及单机单日有效时长等维度明显增长，实现了用户时长的持续集中，也为矩阵内广告、电商、本地生活等业务转化提供基础。

来源：艾瑞咨询 UserTracker 多平台网民行为监测数据库（桌面及智能终端），艾瑞咨询自主研究绘制

平台国内商业化收入向头部内容平台集中
字节系平台商业化收入2023–2025年CAGR约26%，2025年为淘天2倍、腾讯4.6倍、京东6倍；腾讯、京东增
速快但基数低
按可比口径比较，字节系近三年的平台商业化收入保持高绝对值、高增速的特征：2025年平台商业化收入约6500～7000亿元，为2023年的约1.6倍；腾讯商业化收入
两年累计增长45%，但规模仅为字节系的两成左右；京东平台商业化收入两年累计增长约29%至约1100亿元，规模不足字节系的两成；淘天CMR两年累计增长约10%，

美团商业化收入在2025年补贴战下增速回落至个位数。流量份额向字节系集中的同时，商家广告与佣金预算以更快的速度向其集中。
数据来源及说明：公开资料、企业财报、专家访谈，系艾瑞咨询自主研究及整理。

### Page 13

13©2026.8 iResearch Inc.                                                                                                                         www.iresearch.com.cn
用户时长优势转化为更高的广告佣金率
字节系流量以休闲娱乐内容为主，时长更多、与消费更贴合，变现能力显著高于社交工具型与交易型
流量能否变现，首先取决于流量的属性。字节系以约39.8%的用户时长份额（2026年8月Top50APP）获得五大互联网平台近半的商业化收入。显著体现了当前短视频内
容/休闲娱乐型流量的变现能力：
1. 内容创造消费需求：在短视频时代，字节系为代表休闲娱乐型流量占用的用户时长更多，曝光和转化机会更多；这类流量与消费场景天然贴合，用户容易被内容带入
消费场景，广告可以直接创造需求。微信虽凭借用户体量和社交/工具定位同样占据大量时长，但站内流量构成复杂，交易场域分散，站内闭环转化能力较弱；阿里

（淘天）、美团的流量自带购买意图、转化属性强，但用户不会长时间停留，变现主要依靠成交抽佣与搜索广告竞价排名。
2. 广告佣金率差异：横向对比，抖音电商广告佣金率约为其他货架电商平台的3~4倍，其差距主要来自广告端：字节系（抖音）商业化收入中广告占约80%，货币化以
流量曝光为主。
数据来源及说明：1.用户时长份额为2026年8月各集团所属APP在时长TOP50APP月总有效时长中的占比，来源艾瑞咨询 UserTracker，详见第一章；其余为公开资料、企业财报、专家访谈，系艾瑞咨询自主研究及整理

字节系 阿里系 京东 腾讯系
广告佣金率（%） 交易佣金/技术服务费率（%）
内容电商将流量优势转化为业务规模与更高商业费率
• 内容电商路径：从流量聚合到广告变现，GMV是算法流量分发带来的衍生结果，平台核心收入来源于广告，广告收入可在交易完成前兑现，广告佣金率上限更高，
但平台收入高度依赖商家持续投流；
• 货架电商路径：从交易撮合到佣金变现，平台收入直接和商品交易规模绑定，佣金为核心收入来源，变现确定性更强，但整体货币化天花板相对更低。
内容电商依托流量聚合实现广告优先变现；货架电商依靠交易撮合，以佣金作为收入基本盘
数据来源及说明：公开资料、企业财报、专家访谈，艾瑞咨询自主测算及整理

商家在各平台的费率差异
内容电商和货架电商商家成本结构差异明显，流量营销支出是造成成本差距的核心因素
• 内容电商呈现营销驱动的双高成本结构：商家成本主要来自广告营销费、达人/MCN佣金，交易佣金占比偏低，和货架平台佣金水平差异不大；GMV增长高度依赖
付费流量与达人分销拉动，成本集中在流量获取环节，盈利弹性和投放效率深度绑定。
• 货架电商商家成本与交易规模直接挂钩，广告投放为可选增量支出，自然搜索流量贡献更高，整体经营确定性更强，全链路综合成本显著低于内容电商。
数据来源及说明：公开资料、企业财报、专家访谈，艾瑞咨询自主测算及整理

抖音 45%~50% 2.5~3.0 60%~68% 减投后1-3天流量下滑，GMV降幅超30%，恢复投放后3-7天回归正常

京东 5%~10% 2.8~3.3 50%~55% 减投后2-5天流量下滑，GMV下降8%~12%，恢复投放后2-5天回归正常
品牌案例：头部国货彩妆品牌
线上多平台差异化定位，综合成本投入分化
现阶段品牌营销策略在线上3个平台的综合成本率分化显著，高成本源于站内广告投流与达人佣金双重成本叠加，内容场的流量需要持续付费投流，成交依赖达人带货

促进转化，这是内容电商的刚性成本负担，同时，内容场域高度依赖付费流量，减投测试显示，品牌在内容电商平台陷入“不得不投”的经营困境，付费投流从营销
可选项转变为维持经营的必要投入。品牌投放结构调整侧重商城与搜索、压缩泛流量投入，降低对达人与付费流量的依赖，更加关注自然流量与搜索确定性。

17©2026.8 iResearch Inc.                                                                                                                         www.iresearch.com.cn
直播是内容电商将流量变现延伸至商家经营的关键场景
• 交易规模与渗透率：内容电商直播GMV体量更大，直播在整体交易大盘中的渗透率更高；货架电商直播GMV规模相对有限，直播业务在整体交易中的占比偏低；
• 商业化变现能力：内容电商直播广告佣金率、直播综合佣金率更高，直播广告溢价更强；货架电商直播广告佣金水平偏低，直播平台商业化收入占比更小；
• 变现差异来源：直播广告溢价是两类电商直播货币化水平拉开差距的核心，直播广告是直播商业化收入的主要增量来源。
内容电商直播在交易规模、渗透率与商业化变现能力高于货架电商

直播成本结构中，达人佣金与坑位费拉高综合费率，不同平台成本结构差异驱动营销策略调整
某运动服饰头部品牌以全域曝光、拉动线上销售为核心目标，在抖音、淘天等平台的投放持续增长，线上营销的高成本集中于内容场域，因站内投流费与达人佣金双
重叠加，形成内容电商刚性成本负担，品牌采取分渠道差异化运营策略以达到提流量、保利润的目标，一方面缩减泛流量与纯带货达人场次，转向代言人直播与店铺

自播，另一方面加大商城与搜索等确定性流量投入，降低对达人与付费流量的依赖。

抖音本地生活快速追赶，美团到店规模与核销率仍领先
抖音本地生活依托流量优势实现GMV高速扩张，业务规模快速提升；随着平台生态持续完善，订单核销率逐年上行，有效交易规模同步增长。美团到店业务体量保持
行业领先，依托多年本地商家运营积累，核销率长期稳定在高位，交易履约确定性更强。
抖音本地生活依靠流量优势实现规模快速增长，美团到店依托本地商家运营能力，保障高效稳定的交易转化

本地生活：内容种草与搜索货架形成两套差异化模式
抖音本地生活以内容种草为核心，依托短视频、直播激发非计划性消费、创造增量需求，新客获取能力突出，但订单存在一定核销不确定性；美团到店业务属于搜索货架
模式，承接用户已有消费目标的确定性需求，基于LBS位置分发，交易履约稳定性更强。两套流量逻辑也塑造了差异化商家结构：抖音更适配具备内容生产能力、可承担
投放成本的连锁品牌；美团则聚集大量依赖自然搜索流量的中小本地商户。
抖音靠内容创造消费需求，美团靠货架承接确定性消费；两个平台流量机制、用户行为与商家结构明显不同
2025年部分本地生活平台对比：商业模式

本地生活不同平台商业化费率差异
抖音本地生活以广告为商业化第一支柱，本质是将平台内容流量转化为商家的到店引流价值，变现发生在用户决策的前置环节，交易佣金仅为补充性收入；美团到店
以交易佣金为收入基本盘，变现逻辑与线下到店消费关联度更高，因为本地生活业态存在较高的未核销率与退单率，支付口径GMV易高估实际交易规模；核销口径剔
除无效订单干扰，更贴合真实到店消费体量，是衡量平台变现效率与商家经营成本的核心参考。
抖音本地生活以流量广告实现前置变现，美团到店以交易佣金锚定履约收入实现变现

本地生活商家综合成本率对比
1. 商家在抖音本地生活平台的成本呈“达人佣金为核心、广告佣金为辅”的结构，达人佣金占总支出比重较高，本质是“内容种草引流”的经营逻辑，商家GMV高
度依赖达人内容种草撬动到店，成本前置发生在交易决策之前，盈利水平与内容投放效率深度绑定；
2. 商家在美团到店的成本以交易佣金为基础盘，广告与达人投入为增量补充，对应“搜索+交易心智”的到店模式，用户自带消费目的，自然流量贡献更高，成本与
实际到店交易强绑定，经营确定性更强。
抖音本地生活是以达人佣金为核心的内容种草引流模式，美团到店则是以交易佣金为基础的搜索交易心智模式

本地商家案例：某直营餐饮连锁品牌
核销率分水岭，品牌货架化转型，回报确定性与自然流量底盘正相关
某全国直营门店超1000家的餐饮连锁品牌同时在抖音、美团开展团购业务。
抖音与美团的成本结构差异在于达人佣金与站内投流，投流差异在于抖音需持续付费曝光维持热度，美团靠搜索流量承接确定性消费，由此该品牌形成抖音做新客爆

量、美团做复购沉淀的组合运营格局。商家经营高度绑定付费流量，持续投流成为刚性支出，同时商家还需承担核销率风险。随着直播ROI等指标下滑，品牌主动调整
投放策略，缩减直播/内容投放，向搜索货架化转型。
